Entry 1
Category: Arithmetic
Concept: Natural Numbers
Definition: Positive counting numbers starting from 1.
Key Points:
- Fundamental mathematical concept.
- Widely used in science, engineering, AI and programming.
- Frequently tested in competitive exams and interviews.
Example:
Natural Numbers is commonly used in mathematics.
Related Concepts:
Algebra, Geometry, Calculus, Statistics, Linear Algebra, Probability

Category: Calculus, Probability & Statistics
Concept: Integral
Definition: Represents accumulation or area under a curve.
Key Points:
- Fundamental mathematical concept.
- Widely used in science, engineering, AI and programming.
- Frequently tested in competitive exams and interviews.
Example:
∫x dx = x²/2 + C

Algebra, Geometry, Calculus, Statistics, Linear Algebra, Probability
